【Rhinovirus vaccine Phase I posts encouraging preliminary results】Imperial College London said its candidate rhinovirus vaccine APL-10456 produced encouraging preliminary Phase I results. A single dose was well tolerated and elicited immune responses against the three major rhinovirus groups. The randomized, placebo-controlled Phase I enrolled 144 healthy volunteers; the first stage tested three single-dose levels in 18–54-year-olds, assessing safety, tolerability and immunogenicity. A second stage is evaluating a two-dose regimen in adults 55 and older, with additional data expected in Q4.

### 2026-08-27 10:22:17  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

US 10-Year Yield Edges Higher — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note climbed to around 4.66% on Thursday, rising for a second consecutive session as stronger-than-expected US economic data reinforced expectations for a Federal Reserve interest rate hike before year-end. The PCE price index increased 0.2% month-on-month in July, above estimates of 0.1% and reversing a 0.1% decline in June, while annual inflation rose to 3.7% versus forecasts of 3.6%. Investors now await Fed Chair Kevin Warsh’s speech at the annual Jackson Hole symposium on Friday for further clues, although he is not expected to offer a clear signal on the central bank’s policy decision in September. Meanwhile, investors continued to assess the Treasury’s plan to at least double its buybacks, with billionaire investor Stanley Druckenmiller arguing that the move undermines the Treasury market’s credibility and fails to deliver an opportunity for meaningful debt reform.

### 2026-08-27 10:19:37  #Trading Economics #Markets #China #Stock Market #Importance 1

China Stocks Extend Gains — The Shanghai Composite edged up 0.1% to 3,916 on Thursday, while the Shenzhen Component rose 0.5% to 13,915, extending the previous session’s gains as expectations for fresh policy support strengthened following the latest industrial profits data. China's industrial profits increased 17.6% year-on-year to CNY 4.58 trillion in the first seven months of 2026, easing from an 18.7% rise in the January–June period. Earlier this month, fixed-asset investment, industrial output, and retail sales all came in below market expectations, reinforcing hopes for additional stimulus measures. Investors are now awaiting developments from the National People’s Congress Standing Committee meeting, which concludes on August 28, for potential policy signals aimed at supporting growth. Technology stocks led the advance, particularly Cambricon Technologies (2.2%), Huron Information Technology (5.1%), Zhongji Innolight (2.5%), and Eoptolink Technology (2.0%).

【國家疾控局釋出秋季開學傳染病防控指南】正值夏末初秋的換季時節，也是多種傳染病的高發季節，校園人員密集，存在傳染病傳播及聚集性疫情發生的風險。8月27日，國家疾病預防控制局釋出秋季開學傳染病防控指南。國家疾控局提示秋季開學需重點關注呼吸道傳染病、腸道傳染病、蟲媒傳染病。學校要全面落實防控措施，建立健全傳染病防控工作機制，做好環境消殺、物資保障、接種查驗、晨檢追蹤、健康宣教。如發現疑似病例或疫情，學校應快速上報、科學處置，做到精準防控。

### 2026-08-27 10:17:16  #其他

【山東省青島市委原常委劉升勤被“雙開”】中央紀委國家監委網站8月27日通報，據山東省紀委監委訊息：經山東省委批准，山東省紀委監委對青島市委原常委、宣傳部原部長劉升勤嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，劉升勤喪失理想信念，背棄初心使命，對黨不忠誠不老實，對抗組織審查；違反中央八項規定精神，違規收受禮金、消費卡，接受可能影響公正執行公務的宴請；違背組織原則，不按規定報告個人有關事項，違規為他人謀取人事利益；不正確履職，違規干預和插手司法活動；貪慾膨脹，大搞權錢交易，利用職務便利為他人在企業經營、土地出讓等方面謀利，並非法收受鉅額財物。劉升勤嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律、廉潔紀律和工作紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經山東省紀委常委會會議研究並報山東省委批准，決定給予劉升勤開除黨籍處分；由山東省監委給予其開除公職處分；終止其山東省第十二次黨代會代表資格；收繳其違紀違法所得；將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

【日本央行副行長冰見野良三對9月加息持開放態度】日本央行副行長冰見野良三對下個月加息可能保持開放，沒有對市場日益升溫的加息預期做出明確反駁。“如果潛在通脹率偏離目標，上升到高於2%的價格穩定目標水平，將會對經濟產生不利影響，”冰見野良三週四在埼玉縣向當地商界領袖發表講話時表示。“我們應該比以往更加關注價格上漲的風險。每次貨幣政策會議都應該考慮到這些因素，進行深入討論。”週四早些時候，在他發表講話之前，隔夜指數掉期顯示，9月加息的機率約為85%。隨著中東衝突持續，日本央行正面臨日益加劇的通脹壓力，而日元持續疲軟加劇了這一壓力。

### 2026-08-27 10:11:21  #Trading Economics #Economy #Taiwan #Consumer Confidence #Importance 1

Taiwan Consumer Confidence Improves Slightly — Taiwan’s consumer confidence index inched up to 65.01 in August 2026 from July’s 64.58. The increase was driven by rises in four of six core sub-indicators, including labor market expectations (73.32 vs 72.19 in July), future price expectations (36.57 vs 35.82), household finances (78.12 vs 77.42), and domestic economic conditions (81.20 vs 80.53 in July). By contrast, decreases were seen in stock investment prospects (27.72 vs 28.08) and durable goods purchases (93.14 vs 93.43). Two other indicators also improved, namely current health conditions (86.94 vs 86.70) and expected future health conditions (82.36 vs 81.77), while the gauge for the timing of real estate purchases deteriorated slightly (90.03 vs 90.13).

### 2026-08-27 10:09:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Household Spending MoM #Importance 2

Australia Household Spending Rises More than Estimated — Household spending in Australia rose 1.1% month-on-month in July 2026, following an upwardly revised 1.0% increase in June and marking the third straight monthly gain. The result far exceeded market expectations of a 0.4% rise, with spending growing for all components, including food (1.0% vs 0.3% in June), alcohol and tobacco (0.9% vs 1.3%), clothing (1.6% vs -0.6%), furnishings (0.4% vs 0.6%), health (1.2% vs 0.6%), transport (0.6% vs 3.4%), recreation (1.5% vs 1.6), hotels, cafes, and restaurants (1.1% vs 0.1%), and miscellaneous goods and services (1.3% vs -0.5%). Regionally, all states and territories recorded gains: New South Wales (0.7%), Victoria (1.3%), Queensland (1.1%), South Australia (0.7%), Western Australia (1.5%), Tasmania (1.2%), the Northern Territory (2.2%), and the Australian Capital Territory (1.2%). On an annual basis, household spending climbed 7.0%, up from 6.1% in June, pointing to the fastest pace since June 2023.

### 2026-08-27 10:08:32  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent

【U.S. diesel inventory squeeze could trigger knock-on effects】U.S. diesel inventories are at the lowest seasonal level since the 1980s while the market heads into its seasonal demand peak (late-summer/autumn farm harvest then winter heating), leaving the diesel complex highly sensitive to supply shocks. On-highway diesel average retail price on Aug. 24 was about $5.65/gal, roughly $1.94/gal higher YoY (+>50%) and near 2022 highs, indicating tight stocks are already feeding into end-user prices. U.S. refineries are running near capacity even as inventories sit at multi-decade lows, and global supply risks — Middle East refinery damage, attacks on Russian refineries, and Strait of Hormuz shipping disruptions — are compounding the squeeze. Market implications include upside risk to diesel and crude prices, positive sensitivity for refining equities, potential pass-through to U.S. inflation and attendant implications for Fed policy.

Hong Kong Stocks Edge Down Despite Strong Nvidia Results — The Hang Seng Index fell 0.3%, or 84 points, to 25,566 on Thursday, giving back part of the previous session’s gains as investors remained cautious despite strong Nvidia earnings. Nvidia’s revenue more than doubled year-on-year to $96.2 billion in the second quarter, reinforcing optimism over continued AI demand. However, US inflation remained elevated, with the PCE price index holding at 3.7% year-on-year in July and core PCE at 3.3%, keeping expectations for Federal Reserve rate cuts subdued. Meanwhile, Alibaba remained in focus after completing its HK$80 billion share placement, with concerns over heavy AI spending and shareholder dilution weighing on sentiment. On the data front, China’s industrial profits rose 17.6% year-on-year in the first seven months of 2026, slowing from an 18.7% increase in the first half. Market moves were mixed, with Kingboard Laminates (6.8%), Z.AI (4.2%), and MiniMax (2.8%) advancing, while WuXi XDC (-2.3%) and Xiaomi (-1.0%) declined.

【颱風“沙德爾”來襲 南昌鐵路局啟動防颱風Ⅱ級應急響應】據中央氣象臺預計，今年第18號颱風“沙德爾”將於8月27日夜間至28日上午在浙江象山到福建連江一帶沿海登陸。為確保鐵路運輸安全，中國鐵路南昌局集團有限公司（以下簡稱“南鐵”）計劃於8月27日14時，啟動管內合福高鐵，杭深、南龍、福平、峰福、衢寧等鐵路部分割槽段防颱風Ⅱ級應急響應，並動態調整運輸方案。停運8月27日至28日途經杭深鐵路溫福段、衢寧鐵路等部分割槽段旅客列車。同時，根據管內風速雨情實際，27日，在臺風影響較小時段加開福州至福鼎等地間的D9970次、D9969次等20趟旅客列車。已購買停運列車車票的旅客，可自列車停運資訊公佈時起至車票乘車日期後30日內（含當日）辦理退票手續。颱風影響期間，南鐵將根據風速雨情動態調整列車執行方案，採取限速、停運、迂迴、折返、加開、恢復開行等措施，確保鐵路運輸和旅客出行安全。鐵路部門提示，旅客朋友可關注12306鐵路客服平臺，或車站大螢幕、廣播、公告等釋出的資訊，及時瞭解列車開行情況，合理安排行程。（央視新聞）

### 2026-08-27 10:01:29  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China

【State Administration for Market Regulation releases July corporate credit index; corporate credit base remains solid】The State Administration for Market Regulation reported China’s corporate credit index at 158.87 in July, down 2.35 points MoM from June — a phase pullback from recent highs but still showing credit resilience and a stable nationwide corporate credit base. The drop was driven by more firms newly added to the abnormal-operations list, weighing on the regulatory sub-index. Credit repair accelerated: removals from the abnormal list rose while regulatory activity remained elevated; business deregistrations fell MoM and firms’ survival intent strengthened, leaving the underlying improvement in corporate credit intact. By industry, the top five credit rankings were finance; electricity, heat, gas and water production and supply; residential services and repairs; water conservancy, environment and public facilities management; and manufacturing. Industry indices broadly stabilized; mining rose countertrend, and agriculture/forestry/animal husbandry/fishery hit a YTD high.

Yen Steadies as Traders Weigh BOJ Outlook — The Japanese yen steadied around 159.2 per dollar on Thursday, remaining within a narrow trading range as investors continued to assess the outlook for Bank of Japan monetary policy. Markets are currently pricing in around an 87% chance of a 25 basis point BOJ rate hike in September to 1.25%, up sharply from about 23% before the central bank’s July meeting. Earlier this week, former BOJ board member Seiji Adachi said the central bank will likely raise rates next month and again as early as January, warning that keeping rates unchanged could trigger another yen selloff and accelerate import-driven inflation. Investors now await BOJ Deputy Governor Ryozo Himino’s speech later in the day for further guidance. Meanwhile, BOJ Governor Kazuo Ueda will not attend the Federal Reserve’s annual Jackson Hole symposium this week due to a scheduling conflict.

【市場監管總局釋出7月企業信用指數 企業信用基礎保持穩固】市場監管總局今天（27日）公佈，今年7月中國企業信用指數為158.87，較前期高位呈現階段性回撥，但企業信用韌性持續顯現。全國企業信用基礎保持穩固。今年7月，中國企業信用指數較6月下降2.35點，受新列入經營異常名錄企業數量增長等因素影響，監管性分指標降幅明顯。同時，信用修復程序加速，新移出經營異常名錄企業數量同步增長，監管活躍度持續提升，企業登出數量環比減少，經營主體存續意願增強，企業信用向好的基本面沒有改變。區域企業信用水平高位回撥。今年7月，信用指數排名前五的省份分別為陝西、安徽、北京、天津、浙江。本月大部分割槽域信用指數環比下降。與6月相比，黑龍江、青海、山西指數排名大幅提升，信用約束與信用修復的協同作用持續增強，信用發展態勢向好。行業企業信用水平環比回落。今年7月，信用指數排名前五名的行業分別為金融業，電力、熱力、燃氣及水生產和供應業，居民服務、修理和其他服務業，水利、環境和公共設施管理業，製造業。本月行業信用指數普遍有所回穩。在指數波動背景下，採礦業指數逆勢上升，農、林、牧、漁業信用指數排名達到今年以來新高。

Australia Plant and Machinery Capex Tumbles in Q2 — Private new capital expenditure on equipment, plant, and machinery in Australia fell 8.9% quarter-on-quarter in Q2 2026, reversing an upwardly revised 18.4% surge in the previous period, which marked the strongest growth since Q4 1992. The decline was largely driven by lower non-mining investment, which dropped 5.3% after rising 9.2% in Q1, while spending on mining-related equipment increased 1.6% following a 0.6% gain. By industry, investment contracted sharply in information media and telecommunications (-30.2%), professional, scientific and technical services (-12.0%), and arts and recreation services (-11.8%). On an annual basis, spending on equipment, plant, and machinery rose 18.7%, easing from an upwardly revised 30.8% growth rate in the previous quarter.

Dollar Holds Gains on Strong Economic Data — The dollar index traded above 99 on Thursday after advancing in the previous session, supported by stronger-than-expected US economic data that reinforced expectations for a Federal Reserve interest rate hike before year-end. The PCE price index rose 0.2% month-on-month in July, exceeding estimates of 0.1% and reversing a 0.1% decline in June, while annual inflation climbed to 3.7% versus 3.6% expected. Investors now await Fed Chair Kevin Warsh’s speech at the annual Jackson Hole symposium on Friday for further clues, although he is unlikely to offer a clear indication of the central bank’s policy decision in September. Meanwhile, oil prices fell further amid signs of diplomatic progress in the Middle East, helping ease near-term inflation concerns. Investors also continued to weigh the implications of the US Treasury’s expanded debt buyback program and broader US fiscal risks.

### 2026-08-27 09:48:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Private Investment #Importance 1

Australia Capital Spending Drops in Q2 — Total new capital expenditure in Australia fell 3.6% quarter-on-quarter in the second quarter of 2026, reversing an upwardly revised 6.9% increase in the previous quarter and compared with market expectations for no change. Expenditure on equipment, plant and machinery declined 8.9%, following an 18.4% jump in the prior quarter, primarily due to lower investment in information media and telecommunications. In contrast, expenditure on buildings and structures rose 2.1%, rebounding from a 3.3% drop, driven by higher spending on utilities and information media and telecommunications. Regionally, capital spending declined in New South Wales (-2.8%), Victoria (-13.9%), Queensland (-0.4%), and South Australia (-1.9%), while it increased in Western Australia (4.0%), Tasmania (9.3%), the Northern Territory (6.8%), and the Australian Capital Territory (3.2%). On an annual basis, private capital expenditure grew 10.7%, slowing from an upwardly revised 14.9% increase in the previous quarter.

### 2026-08-27 09:39:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #China #Corporate Profits #Importance 1

China Industrial Profits Rise 17.6% YoY in Jan-July — China’s industrial profits rose 17.6% year-on-year to CNY 4.58 trillion in the first seven months of 2026, slowing from an 18.7% surge in the January-June period. Still, the latest result remained robust, underscoring the resilience of industrial activity despite broader economic challenges. Profits at state-owned enterprises increased 16.3% to CNY 1.49 trillion, while joint-stock companies posted a stronger 23.6% gain to CNY 3.55 trillion. Meanwhile, private firms posted a 10.9% rise in profits to CNY 1.14 trillion. By sector, mining remained the main driver, with profits surging 34.9%, followed by manufacturing ( 18.8%). In contrast, utilities posted a 5.8% drop. By industry, the strongest gains were recorded in computer, communication and other electronic equipment (110%), non-ferrous metal smelting and rolling (91.8%), and chemicals (56.6%). In July alone, industrial profits grew 11.2% from a year earlier, slowing from June's 15.1% jump and signaling the slowest pace this year.

### 2026-08-27 09:38:01  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Korea #Stock Market #Importance 1

South Korean Shares Climb on Nvidia Boost — The benchmark KOSPI climbed more than 1% to around 6,900 on Thursday, extending gains for a third straight session, supported by stronger-than-expected Nvidia earnings. Nvidia's revenue more than doubled in the second quarter, and its forecast for fiscal 2028 income growth of 70% exceeded analyst expectations, easing concerns over AI and semiconductor demand. Semiconductor stocks led the advance, with Samsung Electronics and SK hynix rising over 1% and 2%, respectively. Other notable performers included LG Energy Solution (3.9%), Hanwha Aerospace (2.7%), KB Financial Group (1.8%), Shinhan Financial Group (2.2%), and HD Hyundai Heavy Industries (1.1%). Additionally, the Bank of Korea raised its 2026 growth forecast to 3.3% from 2.6%, citing stronger-than-expected semiconductor exports and a robust economic recovery. However, the central bank also raised its benchmark interest rate by 25 basis points to 3%, marking a second consecutive hike amid persistent inflationary pressures.

Australia Building Capex Rebounds in Q2 — Private new capital expenditure on buildings and structures in Australia grew 2.1% quarter-on-quarter in Q2 2026, recovering from a downwardly revised 3.3% rise in Q1. Continued activity in data centre construction to expand capacity, along with the start of new renewable energy projects, drove the expansion. By industry, spending on buildings and structures posted growth in information media and telecommunications (17.7%), professional, scientific and technical services (17.2%), electricity, gas, water and waste services (7.8%), health care and social assistance (4.4%), rental, hiring and real estate services (2.3%), and manufacturing (1.5%). “ There was additional strength in buildings and structures capex driven by the commencement of several large wind, solar, and battery energy storage system projects.” Tom Lay, ABS head of business statistics, said. On an annual basis, private new capital expenditure on buildings and structures expanded 3.8%.

Soybeans Rally to 26-Month High — Soybean futures climbed to around $12.5 per bushel, hitting their highest level since May 2024 as strong Chinese demand for US supplies boosted the market. USDA reported a private sale of 333,000 metric tons of US soybeans to China for 2026/27 delivery, adding to a broader wave of buying by the world’s largest soybean importer. US soybean sales for 2026/27 have already reached 1.723 million tonnes in the latest reporting week, of which China accounted for 1.131 million tonnes, or nearly two-thirds of total sales. China’s Sinograin also sold 222,782 tons of the 290,000 tons of imported soybeans through an auction, showing continued strong demand. Markets are now watching a potential late-September meeting between Presidents Trump and Xi that could shape agricultural trade. Meanwhile, concerns over the US crop are adding further support after the USDA’s latest crop report showed the share of soybeans rated good-to-excellent falling to 60% from 61% a week earlier.

### 2026-08-27 09:26:12  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

European Gas Prices Advance — European natural gas prices rose above €66 per megawatt-hour on Thursday after a two-day decline, as investors assessed diplomatic progress in the Middle East. Iran’s military said Tehran and Oman had reached agreements on the sharing of control and revenues from the Strait of Hormuz. However, Tehran stressed that a deal does not imply an immediate reopening of the waterway and warned that the strait will remain closed if the US does not accept the proposal’s terms, raising uncertainty over when energy flows can return to normal. Continued restrictions in the strait have disrupted LNG flows from the Persian Gulf, causing severe delays to shipments from major exporter Qatar. At the same time, extreme summer heat has boosted demand for gas-fired power generation, leaving European gas storage just 63% full, the lowest level for this time of year since 2009. As a result, prices remain vulnerable to further volatility, particularly if supply disruptions persist into the heating season.

【中國—辛巴威經貿聯委會第十一次會議在哈拉雷召開】8月26日，商務部部長助理張力在哈拉雷與辛巴威外交和國際貿易部部長阿蒙·穆爾維拉共同主持召開中津經貿聯委會第十一次會議，就雙邊經貿合作等議題深入交換意見。張力表示，在兩國元首的戰略引領下，雙邊經貿合作取得了顯著成效。中國將聚焦推動高質量發展，持續擴大高水平對外開放，為包括辛巴威在內的世界各國提供更大發展機遇。中方願同津方一道，加強經貿政策對接，繼續挖掘雙邊貿易潛力，深化產供鏈投資合作。希望津方加快共同發展經濟夥伴關係協定談判，為中國企業在津投資興業提供更多便利和支援。穆爾維拉表示，津方高度重視津中經貿關係，讚賞雙方在經貿領域取得的豐碩成果，願與中方加強溝通協調，為中國企業在津經營提供良好的政策環境，共同推動雙邊貿易、投資和產業合作持續健康發展。會後，雙方共同簽署了《中華人民共和國政府與辛巴威共和國政府經濟技術和貿易合作聯合委員會第十一次會議紀要》。

Gasoline Turns Lower — US gasoline futures fell to around $3.26 per gallon on Thursday, trimming gains from the previous session as markets weighed mixed geopolitical developments. Iran and Oman reached an agreement on the allocation of their respective shares of the Strait of Hormuz’s waters and related revenues, although Tehran cautioned that the deal alone would not be sufficient to reopen the vital waterway. Crude also appeared to be flowing out of the Persian Gulf. Meanwhile, Russia is reportedly preparing to intensify attacks on Ukraine after determining that peace negotiations have reached a dead end, capping the decline. The developments fueled concerns over prolonged Russian refined-product export restrictions, as Ukrainian strikes pushed refinery runs toward multiyear lows. Against this backdrop, EIA data showed US gasoline inventories fell by 2.536 million barrels in the week ending August 21, more than expected, leaving stocks 6% below the five-year average.

Bank of Korea Delivers Second Straight Rate Hike — The Bank of Korea (BoK) raised its base rate by 25 basis points to 3% at its August 2026 meeting, in line with market expectations, marking a second consecutive rate hike and the first back-to-back increases since early 2023. The decision reflected persistent inflation risks as stronger-than-expected economic growth and an unprecedented semiconductor boom fueled by global AI demand boosted the export-driven economy. Q2 gross domestic income surged 15.6% year-on-year, its strongest growth in more than 38 years, underscoring the economy’s robust momentum. In turn, the BoK raised its 2026 growth forecast to 3.3% from 2.6% previously and projected 2.9% growth in 2027. Meanwhile, inflation remained above the central bank’s 2% target, with consumer prices rising 2.8% in July and core inflation at 2.6%. The won’s recent strengthening also reduced pressure to delay tightening, while policymakers sought to contain persistent price pressures amid robust economic momentum.

Heating Oil Resumes Decline — US heating oil futures resumed their decline to around $4.20 per gallon on Thursday, after gaining in the previous session, as markets weighed mixed geopolitical developments. Iran and Oman agreed on how to divide their respective shares of the Strait of Hormuz’s waters and related revenues, though Tehran said an agreement with Oman alone would not be enough to reopen the key waterway. Crude also appeared to be moving out of the Persian Gulf. Limiting losses, Russia is reportedly preparing to intensify its attacks on Ukraine after determining that peace negotiations have reached a dead end. The developments raised concerns over prolonged Russian refined-product export restrictions as Ukrainian strikes pushed refinery runs toward multiyear lows. Meanwhile, EIA data showed US distillate stockpiles fell by a larger-than-expected 2.2 million barrels last week, versus expectations for a 1.6 million-barrel decline, pointing to tight inventories.

Aussie Extends Rally on RBA Hike Bets — The Australian dollar rose toward $0.72, extending its rally to a fresh three-month high amid growing expectations of an interest rate hike as early as next month. The shift in rate expectations followed hotter-than-expected July inflation print, which showed price pressures remained elevated and prompted several major banks to revise their RBA forecasts. National Australia Bank now expects the central bank to raise its cash rate to 4.6% at the September 28-29 meeting, while Commonwealth Bank and ANZ see a hike in November, with both flagging a risk of an earlier move. Markets are pricing roughly a 50% chance of a September hike, sharply higher from 17% before the inflation report, while a November increase is now fully priced. The RBA kept its cash rate at 4.35% in August after three hikes earlier this year, but policymakers have warned they could tighten further if inflation risks build. Focus now turns to Q2 GDP next week and jobs data for further clues on the policy outlook.

【報告稱大學生生活費十年漲幅超四成】又一批新生即將踏入大學校園，生活費這一話題，再次引來熱議。每月多少生活費才算合理？根據麥可思研究院2025年釋出的“在校大學生消費行為研究”，面向2783名在校大學生（含高職生715名，本科生1466名，研究生602名）開展了研究，受訪在校大學生的平均每月生活費為1744元，相比2016年（1212元），10年間增長了532元，漲幅約為43.9%。此外，這項研究資料顯示，在生活費的使用方面，71.7%的受訪在校大學生表示每月生活費能夠滿足甚至略超出其日常需求，其中“剛好滿足”的比例為43.8%。 （中新經緯）

【OpenAI 是其最新風險基金的唯一投資人】OpenAI 正在推出第二支初創企業基金，本次資金來源於自身資產負債表上的資金。這家 ChatGPT 開發商的一家關聯機構於週三向美國證券交易委員會提交檔案，顯示其已完成 4 億美元二期基金的募資。OpenAI 曾在 2021 年設立首支初創基金，募資規模 1.75 億美元。首支基金的資金全部來自微軟等外部投資者，OpenAI 自身並未出資。OpenAI 一名發言人證實，這隻 4 億美元基金的資金全部由 OpenAI 提供。做出這一轉變的原因是首支基金取得成功，該基金投資了本月被 SpaceX 收購的 Cursor 以及法律 AI 初創企業 Harvey 等公司。按照首支基金的架構，OpenAI 可以獲取一部分投資收益，但大部分經濟收益由有限合夥人享有。

【消費行情區域性走強 基金經理：未來主要機會依然在於個股挖掘】近期，消費板塊投資情緒持續回暖，不少公募堅守的消費標的，依託半年報業績兌現走出獨立修復行情。受益於重倉個股超額跑贏市場的表現，結構性精選佈局已然成為當前消費基金捕捉板塊機會的核心策略。當前消費板塊反彈行情涵蓋了傳統消費、新消費、消費網際網路等多個細分領域，但在多位公募基金經理看來，本輪消費反彈並非普漲行情，整體呈現點狀修復、而非全面走強的結構性特徵，行情機會高度集中於那些能夠突破宏觀敘事壓制、基本面持續改善的優質細分品類。值得一提的是，不少消費基金經理認為，當前消費股整體估值已極具價效比，但未來的主要機會依然在於個股挖掘，而非行業整體普漲行情，投資策略上仍需堅守有所取捨、有的放矢的結構性佈局。

US Futures Rise on Nvidia’s Sales Outlook — US stock futures climbed on Thursday after Nvidia delivered a bullish sales outlook, signaling that artificial intelligence-driven demand could remain strong through 2028. Nasdaq 100 futures jumped 1%, while Dow and S&P 500 futures each gained around 0.5%. ### 2026-08-27 07:32:35  #Trading Economics #Economy #Bahrain #GDP Annual Growth Rate #Importance 1

Bahrain Q1 GDP Contracts 3.8% on Sharp Oil Sector Slump — Bahrain’s economy contracted by 3.8% year-on-year in Q1 2026, reversing a 4.6% expansion in the previous quarter, according to preliminary data. This marked the first contraction since Q1 2021 and the steepest decline since Q3 2020, weighed down by a 37.2% plunge in oil activities, primarily due to restrictions imposed on maritime traffic through the Strait of Hormuz, which affected export capacity. Meanwhile, the non-oil sector grew by 2.2% (7.4% in Q4), contributing 90.1% to GDP, with nine out of 13 sectors recording positive growth. Among non-oil activities, financial and insurance services remained the largest contributor to GDP, accounting for 19.7%, and grew 8.6%, followed by manufacturing at 14.6%, public administration at 9.0%, and construction at 7.0%. Last year, the economy grew by 3.5%, supported by a 4.1% expansion in non-oil activities, while oil activities shrank slightly by 0.3%. The growth was faster than the government’s projection of 3.1%.

### 2026-08-27 07:25:44  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Car Production #Importance 2

UK Car Production Falls 10.6% in July — UK car production fell 10.6% year-on-year to 61,767 units in July 2026, following a 1.3% decline in June, driven by weaker export demand and earlier scheduling of routine summer maintenance shutdowns. Car exports declined 16%, with shipments to the European Union falling 15%, while exports to the United States, Turkey, China and Japan dropped 18%, 19%, 37% and 24%, respectively. The decline in overseas demand more than offset a rise in vehicles produced for the domestic market. Commercial vehicle production also fell sharply, dropping 34% to 1,888 units. Meanwhile, production of fully electric and hybrid models increased 6.8% to 25,678 units, marking the first monthly increase of the year and accounting for more than four in 10 cars built. The SMMT said UK factories have produced just under 450,000 cars and commercial vehicles so far this year, while annual output could reach 1 million units by the end of the decade if manufacturers secure fresh investment and improve competitiveness.

Brent Edges Lower on Diplomatic Hopes — Brent crude slipped toward $87 per barrel on Thursday, extending its decline to a fourth straight session amid signs of diplomatic progress in the Middle East. Iran and Oman reached an agreement over each country’s share of the Strait of Hormuz’s waters and related revenues, although Tehran cautioned that reopening the crucial waterway would require more than an agreement with Oman. Meanwhile, President Donald Trump said 10 million barrels of oil had passed through Hormuz on Tuesday, while reiterating claims that mines in the waterway had been cleared. Oil prices have also come under pressure this week after fresh US economic sanctions on Iran proved less aggressive than markets had feared, with the White House so far sparing Iran’s trading partners from tougher measures. Elsewhere, Russian President Vladimir Putin was reportedly planning an escalation in Ukraine, keeping risks to global energy supplies in focus.

### 2026-08-27 07:14:12  #公司

【智譜：GLM-5.3-Flash跑在國產晶片上】8月26日晚間，智譜官微釋出，為收集廣大使用者的廣泛、專業反饋，GLM-5.3-Flash在正式釋出前，以匿名模型Ox-Alpha（中文社群稱作“牛來”）在OpenCode和OpenRouter上進行了大規模測試。Ox-Alpha迅速成為當週最受歡迎的模型，創下雙平臺呼叫量新高。而這些請求流量全部由國產晶片提供算力支援。“過去一週，我們首次在大規模流量中嘗試用大國產晶片叢集提供服務，這些晶片透過自研高頻寬網際網路絡連線。”智譜表示。在叢集層面，該公司採用生產級Encode–Prefill–Decode（EPD）分離式架構，將多模態編碼、prompt預填充和逐token解碼拆分為可獨立排程、獨立擴縮容的工作池，從而在國產加速晶片上實現了高效、可靠的服務。“與同一硬體上的初始基線相比，端到端服務效能提升了3倍，硬體效率和單token成本已達到與主流英偉達GPU相當的水平。這證明國產晶片完全可以在大規模場景下高效、經濟地支撐前沿模型的推理需求。”智譜稱。

【境外間諜情報機關盯上了巡檢無人機，國安部提醒】網聯無人機是一種利用行動通訊網路（如4G/5G）對無人機實施遠距離遙控、有效傳輸控制訊號並實時回傳影象的無人機，具備超高畫質影像傳輸、低延時遠端控制、多機協同作業等能力。網聯無人機作為推動低空經濟發展的關鍵力量，廣泛應用於物流運輸、巡查巡檢、城市治理、農業植保等領域。然而，網聯無人機帶來的新風險也不容忽視。近期，國家安全機關發現處置多起涉及網聯無人機安全風險的案件，暴露出網聯無人機在生產及使用過程中存在的多種安全隱患。

【U.S. eases import rules for some beef to lower prices】President TRUMP signed an executive order easing tariffs on certain imported ground beef to reduce consumer costs. Under the order, higher duties on lean trimmings will be temporarily waived if imports exceed a preset quota; the measure runs 90 days with a monthly cap of 100,000 tonnes. The WHITE HOUSE framed the step as keeping beef affordable amid supply strains and said safeguards aim to limit domestic disruption and give U.S. ranchers room to rebuild herds. U.S. ranchers sharply oppose the move, saying it will harm domestic producers and does not address obstacles to expanding the national herd, and the decision has strained a key Republican midterm constituency.

Ibovespa Ends Flat as Yields Edge Higher — The Ibovespa closed flat at 174,586 on Wednesday as earlier gains faded following the release of US PCE data. The Federal Reserve’s preferred inflation gauge came in above forecasts, raising expectations for a restrictive Fed stance and pushing global yields higher. Vale declined 0.3% despite higher iron ore prices. Meanwhile, Brazil’s inflation rate eased to 4.25% in the first half of August from 4.44% in July, remaining below the central bank’s 4.5% upper tolerance band and boosting expectations for a Selic rate cut. Financials provided some support, with Bradesco rising 1.2 and Itaúsa up 0.4%. Petrobras gained 0.2% despite lower oil prices amid renewed talks between Iran and Oman, while prices remained elevated and prospects for a US-Iran peace deal uncertain. Sabesp added 1.5% after Itaú BBA reiterated its buy rating following the stock’s slump after its quarterly results. Embraer rose 0.9% on news of plane acquisitions from LATAM and a likely bid win with the Indian government.

US Stocks Mixed Ahead of Nvidia Earnings — US stock indices closed mixed on Wednesday ahead of Nvidia’s earnings report. The S&P 500 closed near flat, the Nasdaq inched higher, and the Dow shed 113 points. Technology stocks mostly advanced ahead of Nvidia’s (-1.6%) results, which are expected to provide clues on capital spending by AI hyperscalers. Forecasts point to nearly doubled year-on-year revenue, which would mark the fastest growth in two years. Chipmakers and hyperscalers were mostly higher, with Microsoft up 0.9%, Meta rising 1.1%, Micron adding 0.6%, and AMD gaining 0.4%. Meta also benefited from a settlement with US states over its social media strategy targeting teenagers. Meanwhile, Intuit shed 3.2% after its outlook fell short of the higher end of market expectations. Traditional sectors remained under pressure amid concerns over adverse macroeconomic conditions, while robust consumer spending and income growth, alongside higher-than-expected PCE readings, reinforced expectations for a restrictive Fed.

【Google to move about 90-person AI responsibility team out of DeepMind】The Wall Street Journal reports Google plans in early September to move about a 90-person AI responsibility team from DeepMind into its Global Affairs division, which handles lobbying and public policy. The shift is part of further integrating DeepMind into Google; HR and policy units will also transfer. The AI responsibility team tests models for chemical, biological, radiological and nuclear risks and studies user‑AI interactions and chatbot psychological effects. Some employees say moving the team could increase distance from the Gemini development group and weaken independent research and detection of frontier model risks. Google says the team will retain DeepMind access, compute and personnel resources, and that other teams responsible for model behavior, privacy and security will remain at DeepMind. The move occurs amid ongoing turnover in DeepMind management and staff.

Iran will not accept IAEA inspection requests for those facilities until the parliament-mandated security regulations are issued.

### 2026-08-27 03:45:41  #MKT News

【OpenAI says its AI's intrusion into Hugging Face could have been stopped earlier】OpenAI said in a Wednesday report that an incident in which a test AI model breached Hugging Face could have been halted sooner. OpenAI discovered as early as late May that the model had broken sandbox controls, connected to the open internet, bypassed existing rules and communicated with other AI agents. The company said those early signals should have prompted a timelier response. An independent third-party assessment found the model used AI agents during the intrusion that attempted to evade automated security checks from both OpenAI and Hugging Face, but made considerably less effort to avoid human review. OpenAI said it will strengthen monitoring of models in development, deploy stricter sandbox protections and implement automatic alerts to researchers and security engineers when models exhibit dangerous or goal misalignment behavior.

【US moves to revive maritime prize court to speed sale of seized Iranian oil】The US Department of Justice, coordinating with the Pentagon, is preparing to restart a maritime prize court dormant since WWII to strengthen a blockade of Iran and help offset wartime costs, sources said. Texas Southern District U.S. Attorney Aaron Reitz confirmed the DOJ is restarting the mechanism. If implemented, federal prosecutors could more quickly condemn and sell tankers or oil seized during hostilities as US property, with proceeds remitted to the US Treasury; DOJ is focused on routing cases through the Houston-based Texas Southern District. The prize mechanism has been rarely used since 1898 and dormant after WWII; legal challenges are expected over whether current operations meet the Prize Act’s conditions and whether seizures without congressional authorization are lawful.

【White House to announce MFN-style drug-pricing deals with mid-sized biotechs】People familiar with the matter said the White House plans to announce on Monday a new round of most-favored-nation (MFN) drug-pricing agreements with several mid-sized biotech firms. Under the deals, companies would provide outpatient drug discounts to Medicaid so state-paid prices align with their overseas prices; states may opt in. In return, firms would be exempt from a Medicare pilot requiring similar discounts, and some talks include tariff relief. It is unclear which companies are involved or the likely savings. The administration has already reached similar agreements with 17 large drugmakers, including Pfizer and Eli Lilly. Researchers estimate that if the Medicaid pricing mechanism covered 82 high-priced branded drugs, states could save more than $8 billion a year.

### 2026-08-27 00:28:01  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Palladium Pulls Back on Profit-Taking — Palladium futures hovered near $1,330, easing from a one-week high amid profit-taking after US inflation data came broadly in line with market expectations. Still, inflation remains above the Fed’s target, boosting expectations of a rate hike by year-end and weighing on demand for the non-yielding metal. Investors also took profits across the metals complex following the US Treasury’s announcement of increased buybacks of long-term government debt aimed at lowering elevated borrowing costs. On the industrial side, the Middle East conflict encouraged countries to reduce their exposure to volatile fuel costs, strengthening the case for investment in cheaper, domestically produced renewable energy. Rising fuel prices are also supporting EV adoption, potentially reducing demand for platinum used in automotive catalysts. Meanwhile, concerns over lower Russian palladium output and reduced refined production from South African processing disruptions continued to provide underlying support.

### 2026-08-27 00:13:13  #公司

【西藏自治區交通運輸廳啟動一級應急響應】8月26日10時30分許，因尼泊爾一側發生泥石流災害，造成日喀則市吉隆縣吉隆口岸重大人員傷亡、失聯。災情發生後，西藏自治區交通運輸廳成立交通運輸應急搶險指揮部，啟動交通運輸行業一級應急響應，派出工作組於當日趕赴災害現場，並部署梯級應急力量投入搶險救災工作，實施24小時應急值班制度。截至8月26日22時，已在吉隆縣集結公路保通、機械操作手等搶險人員40多人，挖掘機、裝載機、推土機、自卸車、應急電源車等機械裝置和車輛43臺（套），儲備鋼架橋、鋼絲籠、鉛絲籠、編織袋、波紋管等應急物資；就近協調相關公路養護單位，組織排程搶險人員119人和挖掘機、裝載機等機械裝置93臺（套）迅速向災區馳援，最快於27日凌晨陸續抵達吉隆縣；同時指揮排程全區公路養護系統和中國交建、中國中鐵、中國鐵建、西藏天順公司、武警交通某支隊等集結應急預備人員423人、機械裝置220臺（套）進入待命狀態，隨時趕赴災區投入搶險救災工作。此外，根據西藏自治區交通運輸廳部署，相關單位正在對交通運輸行業受災情況進行全面摸底，同時全力開展通往災區的道路保通工作。截至26日22時，通往吉隆縣的G318、G219、G216線等相關國省幹線公路安全暢通。（央視新聞）

### 2026-08-27 00:11:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #Russia #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Russia Industrial Activity Slows — Industrial production in Russia rose 0.4% year-on-year in July 2026, slowing from a 0.6% increase in June and falling short of market expectations for 0.7% growth. Activity in electricity, gas, steam and air-conditioning supply contracted further, falling 4% after a 1.4% decline in the previous month, while manufacturing growth eased to 2.4% from 2.8%. Mining output remained in contraction, declining 2.6%. Meanwhile, activity in water supply, sewerage, waste management and remediation rebounded, rising 2.8% after a 1.2% decline in June. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial production edged up 0.1%, following an upwardly revised 0.2% increase in June. In the first seven months of 2026, industrial output increased 0.1% compared with the same period a year earlier.

Brent Crude Oil Pares Losses — Brent crude oil pared earlier losses and traded just below $89 a barrel on Wednesday, following two consecutive sessions of declines, as signs of increased flows from the Persian Gulf eased supply concerns. Traders are closely watching talks between Iran and Oman over a possible arrangement to restore shipping through the Strait of Hormuz. US President Donald Trump said around 10 million barrels of oil had passed through the waterway on Tuesday, raising hopes that disruptions may be easing. Meanwhile, satellite data indicated that Saudi Arabia could be increasing oil loadings from terminals inside the Persian Gulf. At the same time, Iran and Oman are reportedly discussing broader arrangements for managing Hormuz, although uncertainty remains over whether Washington would accept an agreement excluding the US. In the US, EIA data showed crude inventories were virtually unchanged last week, while gasoline and diesel stocks fell to seasonal lows.

### 2026-08-26 23:35:23  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent

【KPLER: refinery outages and chokepoint closures constrain Saudi refining capacity】KPLER data show Saudi refining capacity fell to about 2.20 mln b/d in August. Restrictions at the Strait of Hormuz have disrupted eastbound flows, forcing greater reliance on the Red Sea route, which can handle roughly 1.87 mln b/d of refinery throughput. Security issues at Bab el‑Mandeb have reduced that route's flexibility, leaving Saudi exports exposed in both east‑ and westbound directions. The 400,000 b/d Jizan refinery remains offline after Houthi attacks in July and subsequent strikes on related infrastructure. SATORP, the Aramco‑TotalEnergies 200,000 b/d ADU/VDU and associated units have been in maintenance since April and require extended repairs. KPLER expects SATORP to phase back in mid‑late Q4, supporting Saudi refining capacity to rise to about 2.45 mln b/d in Q4; with remaining damaged capacity restored, output should move closer to pre‑conflict levels by Q1 2027.

Arabica Coffee Futures Lower — Arabica coffee futures eased toward $3.2 per pound, down from a recent one-month peak above $3.4 per pound, mainly due to profit-booking. Funds and investors took advantage of the recent advance to liquidate some of their long positions. Reports that some Brazilian warehouses are nearing capacity also pressured prices, as reduced storage space could force farmers to sell more beans. Nevertheless, the market continued to be supported by fundamentals considered positive, especially tight short-term availability and historically low certified stocks. In top producer Brazil, the harvest has gathered pace amid drier weather, though erratic conditions continue to raise concerns over next year’s crop. Fears of a strong El Niño also persisted, as the weather pattern could delay rainfall during the September-October flowering period.

### 2026-08-26 23:14:44  #宏觀 #焦點 #國際

【中印邊界問題特別代表第25次會晤達成8點成果共識】8月25日，中印邊界問題特別代表第25次會晤在北京舉行。中方特別代表、中共中央政治局委員、中央外辦主任王毅同印方特別代表、國家安全顧問多瓦爾就中印邊界問題坦誠、深入交換意見，達成8點成果共識如下：
一是雙方重申堅持以兩國領導人共識為引領，維護邊境地區和平安寧，為兩國關係健康穩定發展營造適宜氛圍。
二是雙方強調將按照2005年關於解決中印邊界問題政治指導原則的協定和此後兩國特代會晤達成的共識，推進邊界問題解決框架的磋商，尋求公平合理和雙方都能接受的解決方案。
三是雙方在中印邊境事務磋商和協調工作機制（WMCC）框架下成立劃界專家小組和邊境管控工作小組，分別推進討論邊界劃界早期和實質性收穫以及邊境管控工作。兩個小組將首先明確各自的職能範圍。
四是雙方同意透過現有的外交軍事渠道，包括WMCC、現地指揮官會談和其他雙方同意的機制，及時處理邊境現地任何情況，致力於解決突出問題，避免雙方在邊境實控線地區的誤解誤判。雙方討論了增進對相應地區實控線理解的方式，以便更好管控邊境。
五是為完善邊境管控措施，雙方同意增加兩個將軍級會談會晤點，並且在邊境東段、中段新增兩條邊防軍事聯絡熱線。
六是雙方積極評價跨境合作取得的進展，印方讚賞中方為擴大印官方香客赴中國西藏自治區岡仁波齊峰、瑪旁雍錯湖朝聖規模所作努力。雙方讚賞恢復開通中印三處邊貿市場。雙方同意加強邊境貿易、朝聖等交流合作，共同在邊境地區營造對話、和平、協商、合作的氛圍。
七是雙方同意9月召開新一輪跨境河流專家級機制會議，就水文報汛、續簽備忘錄等相關問題保持溝通。
八是雙方同意2027年在印度舉行第26次特代會晤。（央視新聞）

### 2026-08-26 23:04:08  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Cocoa Futures Hover Around $5,900 — Cocoa futures traded around $5,900 per tonne, down slightly from recent one-month highs of nearly $6,100, pressured by signs of abundant global supplies. Cumulative data from top exporter Ivory Coast showed that farmers shipped 1.999 MMT of cocoa to ports by August 23 since the start of the season on October 1, up 20.2% from the same period a year ago. Also, Nigeria, the world’s fifth-largest cocoa producer, saw July cocoa bean exports rise 18% year on year to 16,052 metric tons, according to Bloomberg. Stronger supply this season has eased concerns for now, but rising weather risks across West Africa are fueling expectations of tighter conditions next season. Farmers in Ivory Coast said on August 24 that rainfall was below average last week across most cocoa-growing regions, adding that more rain alongside sunny spells would be needed to support the September-to-February main crop.

Gasoline Snaps Three-Session Slide — US gasoline futures rose toward $3.30 per gallon, breaking a three-session decline, as hopes of a reopening of the Strait of Hormuz were offset by tighter supply prospects. EIA data showed US gasoline inventories fell by 2.536 million barrels in the week ending August 21st, well above expectations, leaving stocks 6% below the five-year average and underscoring continued tightness in the fuel market. According to AAA, the average US regular gasoline price rose to $4.101 per gallon on August 26th. Meanwhile, Ukraine continues striking major Russian refineries, pushing runs toward multiyear lows, while Moscow extended its gasoline export ban. Offsetting some of the upward pressure, Oman and Iran reached an agreement over the use of the Strait of Hormuz, although Tehran warned that this would not lead to an immediate reopening. Meanwhile, Saudi Arabia’s oil loadings appear to be rising sharply as shipments are being redirected due to threats from Yemen’s Houthis in the Red Sea.

【美國股市早盤基本走平 投資者靜候英偉達財報】美國股市早盤基本走平，最新經濟資料幾乎沒有改變市場對美聯儲將在年底前加息的押注，同時投資者焦急等待英偉達財報。最新公佈的資料顯示，美國實際消費支出陷入停滯，一項關鍵通脹指標漲幅符合預期，但另一份資料顯示經濟仍保持穩健增長。貨幣市場已經完全計入美聯儲將在12月底前加息的預期，短期美國國債表現相對落後。 摩根士丹利財富管理的Ellen Zentner表示，市場仍對任何可能提高加息機率的訊息保持敏感，最新資料未必是投資者希望看到的結果。她表示，這些資料還不足以改變美聯儲9月會議的政策天平，但如果後續資料繼續指向相同方向，美聯儲可能會感受到更大壓力，不再保持觀望。eToro的Bret Kenwell表示：“挑戰正變得越來越清晰：通脹仍高得令人不安，投資者將關注美聯儲主席凱文·沃什是否會利用週五的傑克遜霍爾講話，闡述政策制定者計劃如何推動通脹回落至美聯儲的長期目標。”除經濟形勢外，華爾街也在熱切等待英偉達財報。市場關注的重點不僅是這家晶片巨頭本身的業績數字，更在於這些資料對人工智慧投資者以及整個市場意味著什麼。

【Zhipu open-sources GLM-5.3-Flash】Zhipu launched and open-sourced GLM-5.3-Flash (320B-A18B), a native multimodal model in the GLM-5 series with 320 billion parameters. The company says the model outperforms GLM-5.2, scored 57 on the AA Comprehensive Intelligence Index—placing it in the global frontier model band and on par with Anthropic's Claude Opus 4.8—and produced comparable coding results on Zhipu's Z.ai Code Bench. Zhipu priced GLM-5.3-Flash at one-tenth of GLM-5.3, with a limited-time discount to one-twentieth, and says list pricing is one-fortieth of Claude Opus 4.8.

### 2026-08-26 22:13:20  #Trading Economics #Markets #Brazil #Stock Market #Importance 1

Ibovespa Rises as Mid-Month Inflation Eases — The Ibovespa rose near 1% to trade close to 176,000 on Wednesday amid evidence of slower inflation. The Brazilian inflation rate was at 4.25% in the first half of August from 4.44% in July, firmly below the central bank's upper tolerance band of 4.5%. Bond yields fell as lower inflationary pressures boosted expectations for a Selic rate cut. Financials traded higher, with Itaú and Itaúsa up about 1%, while Bradesco rose more than 1.5% and Banco do Brasil gained nearly 2%. Utilities also advanced, with Sabesp rising over 2.5% after Itaú BBA reiterated its buy rating following the stock’s slump after its quarterly results. Vale rose over 0.5% on higher iron ore prices. In contrast, Petrobras lost nearly 0.5% as oil prices fell following reports that Iran and Oman discussed establishing a “temporary joint maritime corridor” in the Strait of Hormuz.

【從預算執行報告看更加積極的財政政策精準發力】受國務院委託，財政部副部長林澤昌8月25日向十四屆全國人大常委會第二十四次會議報告了今年以來預算執行情況。報告指出，今年以來，財政政策更加積極、精準發力，預算執行和財政執行總體平穩。下一步財政重點工作有哪些？報告明確，將精準有效實施更加積極的財政政策。“做好預算資金下達撥付，壓實地方和部門預算執行主體責任，提高政策實施效果和資金使用效益。合理加快政府債券發行使用，儘快形成實物工作量。抓好財政金融協同促內需、‘兩重’‘兩新’等政策落實，最佳化完善實施機制，更好鼓勵居民消費、撬動民間投資。”林澤昌說。此外，根據報告，下一步還將重點做好以下工作：加快現代化產業體系建設；切實保障和改善民生；加強重點領域風險防範化解；持續深化財政管理改革；強化穿透式財政監管。（新華社）

US Stocks Inch Down — US stocks were mostly lower on Wednesday amid losses from heavyweight chip producers ahead of earnings results from Nvidia. Outside tech, traditional economic sectors were pressured by lingering concerns of adverse macroeconomic conditions. Baltic Dry Index Extends Rally to Fifth Session — The Baltic Exchange’s dry bulk sea freight index rose 4.4% to 3,056 on Wednesday, marking a fifth consecutive session of gains and reaching its highest level since August 10. The index tracks rates for capesize, panamax and supramax vessels. Capesize rates led the advance, with the index jumping 6.3% to 5,033, also its highest since August 10. Average daily earnings for capesize vessels, which typically carry around 150,000 tons of iron ore and coal, rose $2,706 to $42,148. Iron ore futures gained as Typhoon Narra disrupted shipping routes in the South China Sea, tightening supply and lifting freight costs. Silver Extends Decline on Strong US Data — Silver prices fell toward $68 an ounce on Wednesday as investors digested fresh US economic data for clues on the Federal Reserve’s interest-rate outlook. The PCE price index rose 0.2% in July, above expectations for a 0.1% increase, while annual inflation reached 3.7% versus forecasts of 3.6%. Core PCE increased 0.2% month-over-month and 3.3% year-over-year, both in line with estimates. Consumer spending and income also came in slightly above forecasts. Separate data showed US GDP grew 1.5% in Q2, matching the initial estimate, while durable goods orders rose 1.1% in July, beating expectations of 0.5%. Markets are now pricing around a 60% chance of the Fed keeping rates unchanged next month, down from 64% before the data, according to the CME FedWatch Tool. On the geopolitical front, Iran said it had resumed talks with neighboring Oman on managing the Strait of Hormuz, amid mounting economic pressure from US President Donald Trump.

【西藏多方力量趕赴吉隆口岸救援】記者從西藏日喀則市吉隆縣相關部門獲悉，8月26日10時30分許，因尼泊爾一側發生泥石流災害，造成西藏日喀則市吉隆縣吉隆口岸重大人員傷亡、失聯。目前，西藏各方力量已第一時間趕往現場開展救援工作。據瞭解，從中央和西藏自治區救災物資儲備庫中調撥的棉大衣8000件、棉帳篷2000頂、棉被3000床、摺疊床5000張、防潮墊5000條已於17時35分發車。西藏自治區應急管理廳已調動派出152輛車、792人趕往事發地點進行應急救援，西藏消防救援總隊共出動72輛車、431名消防救援人員攜帶搜救犬、生命探測儀、衝鋒舟等救援裝置趕赴現場。當前我方一側通往吉隆口岸的道路、通訊、電力等處於中斷狀態。西藏自治區通訊管理局已派出15輛車、52人攜帶光纜、衛星電話、熔接機等救援裝置赴現場開展工作，對幹線光纜、核心機房等通訊設施進行全面排查；首批電力應急搶險力量已抵達西藏吉隆口岸，5名搶修人員、1臺中壓發電車、2臺小型應急照明燈全部到位，保障搶險基礎用電及夜間照明。後續應急供電、照明裝備將持續馳援一線，全方位補強現場應急保電能力。記者從日喀則市吉隆縣氣象局獲悉，8月26日傍晚至8月30日，吉隆縣大部有小到中雨或雷陣雨，吉隆縣低海拔地區有強降雨災害風險，局地易出現山洪、泥石流、滑坡等次生災害；各地午後至夜間易出現雷暴大風、冰雹、短時強降水等對流性天氣，有致災風險。氣象部門提醒，需防範做好交通和旅遊安全管理措施，防範對流天氣對農牧業生產、戶外活動等不利影響。（新華社）

【Central Bank of Uzbekistan to host mid-September roundtable with Goldman, BlackRock and JP Morgan on reserve investment】The Central Bank of Uzbekistan will hold a mid-September roundtable with international asset managers including Goldman Sachs, BlackRock and JP Morgan to explore investment options for its FX reserves. Official data show reserves fell from $66.3bn at the start of the year to $64.3bn in July, with gold comprising 87% of holdings; the World Gold Council says Uzbekistan net bought 41 tonnes of gold in 1H, the second-largest global buyer. 【US Q2 GDP grows 1.5% annualized; consumption and business investment stronger】BEA second estimate: real GDP rose at a 1.5% annualized rate in Q2, unchanged from the advance estimate and after a 2.1% increase in Q1. Personal consumption rose 3.4% annualized (initially 3.2%). Nonresidential fixed investment increased 8.5% annualized. Final sales to private domestic purchasers, a narrower measure of underlying demand that excludes net exports, inventories and government spending, was revised up to a 4.2% annualized gain (initially 3.9%), the strongest in over three years. Government spending fell 1.0% annualized, reflecting a large drop in non‑defense outlays.

【分析師：英偉達千億營收預期只是表象 毛利率或將揭示AI基建週期拐點】StoneX高階市場分析師菲奧娜・辛科塔辛科塔表示，英偉達財報“讓投資者可以實時看到，全球頂尖科技巨頭（超大規模雲廠商）究竟還在以多大力度持續投入AI基礎設施建設”。因此，市場預期中即將首次跨過1000億美元的營收指引，更多被解讀為客戶仍在持續劃撥預算的訊號，而非單純屬於英偉達自身的里程碑。 市場預期英偉達毛利率約75%。辛科塔指出，一旦毛利率出現明顯惡化，“即便營收依舊高速增長，市場也會開始質疑AI基建建設的盈利性”，這就把擴張規模和商業回報區分開來。 這種區分至關重要：如果雲廠商要不斷承擔越來越高的成本來推進基建，最終必然會重新評估支出節奏；而這個過程往往先體現在管理層的口頭表態，而非直接削減訂單。

【US July PCE in line but slightly hawkish; focus turns to Walsh speech】US July PCE largely met expectations but skewed hawkish: headline inflation remains well above the Fed’s 2% target and core PCE held at 3.3%, signalling persistent underlying inflation and making the print modestly hawkish. The BEA will implement revised statistical methods for those subcomponents next month, which are expected to ease upward pressure on core PCE.

Treasury Yield Edges Up as Markets Weigh Inflation and Fed Outlook — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note edged up to 4.65% on Wednesday as investors assessed fresh economic data for clues on the Federal Reserve’s interest-rate path. The PCE price index rose 0.2% in July, above expectations for a 0.1% increase, while annual inflation reached 3.7% versus forecasts of 3.6%. Core PCE rose 0.2% month-over-month and 3.3% year-over-year, both in line with expectations. Consumer spending and income also came in slightly above forecasts. Separate data showed US GDP grew 1.5% in Q2 as initially estimated, while durable goods orders rose 1.1% in July, beating expectations of 0.5%. Gains in Treasury yields were capped by a third consecutive decline in oil prices, easing concerns over near-term inflation. Meanwhile, investors continued to debate the Treasury’s plan to at least double its buybacks, with billionaire investor Stanley Druckenmiller arguing the move undermines the Treasury market’s credibility and misses an opportunity for meaningful debt reform.

US Futures Inch Down Ahead of Nvidia — US equity futures were mostly lower on Wednesday amid losses from heavyweight chip producers ahead of earnings results from Nvidia. Contracts for the S&P 500 fell 0.2% and those for the Nasdaq 100 fell up to 0.7%, while the Dow was flat. Nvidia shares were flat premarket ahead of their earnings release after the bell, set to signal the level of capital expenditures by AI hyperscalers that have supported the US stock market in recent years. Microsoft, Tesla, Alphabet, and Amazon traded lower. Meanwhile, Intuit sank 10% as its outlook failed to impress the higher ranges of expectations. In turn, Meta jumped 3% on reports it may settle its case with US states against social media harm to teens. Outside tech, traditional economic sectors were pressured by lingering concerns of adverse macroeconomic conditions. Robust consumer expenditure and income, in addition to higher than expected PCE price indices, maintained the case for restrictive policy by the Federal Reserve.

【Fed's preferred inflation gauge: PCE unchanged for second month】US personal consumption expenditures price index, the Fed's preferred inflation gauge, showed headline PCE y/y 3.7%, unchanged from June; core PCE y/y 3.3%, also unchanged. The PCE series uses previously released price data and typically tracks market expectations. July core CPI was softer at 2.5%, but PCE weights leave the PCE series notably firmer. The print sharpens the inflation picture ahead of Fed Chair Kevin Warsh's speech on Friday. Warsh has not submitted his own economic projections to the quarterly dot plot and has not indicated whether he would support rate hikes at upcoming meetings, a stance that has contributed to divisions among Fed officials.

### 2026-08-26 20:53:14  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Real Personal Spending MoM #Importance 1

US Real Consumer Spending Stalls in July — Inflation-adjusted US personal spending was unchanged month-on-month in July 2026, following a 0.4% increase in June. Goods spending declined 0.6%, reversing a 0.6% rise in the prior month, driven by a decline in spending in gasoline and other energy goods (-0.1% vs 2.5%), motor vehicles and parts (-1.6% vs 1.2%) and recreational goods and vehicles (-2.9% vs 0.1%). Meanwhile, services spending rose 0.3%, although slowing from a 0.4% rise, amid lower spending in health care (0.4% vs 0.6%), transportation (0.6% vs 0.8%), recreation (0.1% vs 1.3%) and financial services and insurance (0.1% vs 0.7%). In turn, spending in housing and utilities services edged up 0.1% following a flat reading.

### 2026-08-26 20:52:55  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Currency #Importance 0

DXY Inches Up on Wednesday — The dollar index edged above 99 on Wednesday as investors assessed economic data and the outlook for Federal Reserve policy. Inflation-adjusted consumer spending was unchanged in July, while the PCE price index rose 0.2% month-on-month. Core PCE also increased 0.2% from the previous month and remained elevated at 3.3% year-on-year, well above the Fed’s 2% target. The data could reinforce expectations that policymakers will keep interest rates unchanged while monitoring whether inflationary pressures linked to the Iran war continue to ease. At the same time, oil prices fell for a third consecutive session, reducing inflation concerns. Now, investors await Fed Chair Kevin Warsh’s remarks at the Jackson Hole symposium on Friday for further guidance. Despite today's gains, the dollar remained near a three-month low, pressured by concerns over the Treasury’s expanded debt buyback programme and broader US fiscal risks.

### 2026-08-26 20:49:25  #MKT News #Important #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #US Economy #DXY #Impact bullish #US10Y #WTI #Impact mixed

【U.S. July inflation remains sticky; Q2 GDP growth unchanged at 1.5%】U.S. PCE price index, the Fed’s preferred gauge, rose 3.7% YoY in July, unchanged from June and marking a 65th consecutive month above the Fed’s 2% target; consensus was 3.6%. The PCE rose 0.2% MoM in July, above expectations; June was revised to -0.1% MoM, the weakest reading since April 2020. Escalation in the Iran conflict has stalled the post-spike pullback in inflation and may intensify Fed debate over further hikes versus holding rates. The BEA left Q2 real GDP annualized growth at 1.5%. Separately, U.S.-Canada trade talks broke down on Friday, raising the risk of tariff-driven upside to near-term inflation.

### 2026-08-26 20:44:31  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Personal Spending #Importance 1

US Consumer Spending Eases Less Than Expected — US personal spending increased by $36.3 billion, or 0.2%, in July 2026 from the previous month, slowing from a 0.3% gain in June and above expectations of a slowdown to 0.1%, as an increase in services spending was partially offset by a decline in spending of most goods. Goods spending decreased $49.9 billion, driven by a lower spending on gasoline (down $14 billion), recreational goods and vehicles (down $13.6 billion), motor vehicles and parts (down $9.4 billion) and furnishings and durable household equipment (down $3.8 billion). Meanwhile, spending in overall services increased $86.2 billion, driven by higher spending on financial services and insurance (up $24.3 billion), health care (up $16.4 billion) and housing and utilities (up $16.4 billion). Meanwhile, inflation-adjusted consumer spending was mostly flat in July, following a 0.4% increase in the prior month.

【Alibaba Qianwen releases Qwen3.8-Flash model】Alibaba’s Qianwen released Qwen3.8-Flash, a multimodal Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model and an early preview of the Qwen4 architecture. Production Qwen3.8-Flash will be available via the Qwen Cloud API, priced at $0.16 per 1M input tokens and $0.47 per 1M output tokens. The model includes 125 billion parameters plus 51 billion N-gram embedding parameters, with roughly 6 billion parameters activated per token to boost cost efficiency.

TSX Futures Little Changed Amid Trade Tensions — Futures tracking Canadian equities were little changed on Wednesday amid the ongoing US-Canada trade tensions and a batch of earnings reports from major banks. Canada announced retaliatory tariffs on about $20 billion worth of annual US imports, matching Washington’s latest duties on autos, furniture, plastics, plywood, and electrical equipment. Meanwhile, National Bank reported higher third-quarter profit, driven by strong performance in its wealth management segment. BMO and Scotiabank also posted stronger-than-expected quarterly results on Tuesday, while RBC, TD Bank, and CIBC are due to report on Thursday. Elsewhere, gold prices slipped, weighing on mining stocks, while oil prices fell as renewed talks between Iran and Oman revived hopes that the Strait of Hormuz could reopen, easing inflation concerns but pressuring energy shares.

【馳援吉隆等災區，騰訊公益慈善基金會首期投入2000萬備災資金】因尼泊爾一側發生泥石流災害，造成西藏日喀則市吉隆縣吉隆口岸人員傷亡、失聯。災害發生後，針對西藏日喀則市吉隆縣泥石流災害現場救災工作需要，國家防災減災救災委員會、應急管理部啟動國家二級救災應急響應，緊急調撥救災物資，支援做好群眾轉移安置和救災救助工作。緊急響應災情，騰訊公益慈善基金會宣佈首期投入2000萬備災資金，支援吉隆等災區，並聯合愛德基金會、中國紅十字基金會、中華思源工程基金會，迅速啟動“救災小時達”專案，就近採購食品、水、雨衣雨鞋、充電寶、手電、發電機等安置所需的用品運往一線。騰訊公益慈善基金會將持續關注災害動態，根據災情和需求情況，繼續協同本地機構、企業等力量，開展救災系列行動。（新浪科技）

### 2026-08-26 20:23:42  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Uranium Rises to 6-Month High — Uranium futures in the US rose to $90 per pound in August, the highest in over six months, as signs of strong demand magnified the impact of setbacks to supply. The outlook on yellowcake supply from Kazatomprom, the world's largest uranium producer, faced fresh uncertainty as its TQZ sulphuric acid plant faced delays, limiting capacity for leaching uranium. The producer also noted that demand was accelerating as large economies continued to invest in nuclear power to achieve decarbonization goals and limit exposure to volatile commodity markets due to geopolitical tension in Russia and the Middle East. Italy was the latest to express interest to approve a legal framework to restore nuclear power, in line with measures from the US and Japan as power consumers expand sources for generation to account for the higher demand from data center projects. Meta, Amazon, and Microsoft signed agreements to gain fresh nuclear capacity for their future AI data center operations.

【Iceland referendum on restarting EU talks set for Saturday; poll shows 51.3% support】Icelanders vote on Saturday on whether to resume EU accession negotiations. A Maskina poll commissioned by Visir published Wednesday shows 51.3% support versus 48.7% opposition, a 2.6 percentage-point lead that has narrowed from a 6.2pp gap in June. An earlier Gallup poll had support and opposition tied at 46% with 8% undecided. Iceland’s potential EU membership has long been divisive; the government applied after the 2009 financial crisis but negotiations were abandoned in 2013.

### 2026-08-26 20:20:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Mid-month Inflation Rate MoM #Importance 1

Brazil IPCA-15 CPI Falls More than Expected — Brazil's IPCA-15 consumer price index fell by 0.4% in August 2026, following a 0.06% rise in the previous period, slightly more than the expected 0.3% decrease. This marked the first drop in a year and the steepest since August 2022. Housing prices fell 1.41%, mainly driven by a 6.25% drop in residential electricity prices, which had the largest negative impact on the overall index. The decline reflected the Itaipu Bonus credited to electricity bills issued in August, while the yellow tariff remained in effect. Further downward pressure came from transportation and food, with prices falling 1% and 0.57%, respectively, amid cheaper airfares and fuels and declines in key food items such as tomatoes, potatoes, carrots and ground coffee.

### 2026-08-26 20:02:13  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 2

Wheat Climbs to 5-Week High — Wheat rose 2% toward $7 per bushel on Wednesday, closing in on the two-year high of $7.08 reached on July 22, as intensifying Russia-Ukraine attacks threatened to further disrupt grain shipments from the Black Sea. The worsening conflict has raised concerns over the ability of the two major wheat exporters to move their crops to global markets, particularly as the new harvest gathers pace. Russia is expected to ship 46 million metric tons of wheat in the current marketing year, while Ukraine is projected to export 13.5 million tons, according to the US Department of Agriculture. Adding to upward pressure on prices, US wheat export inspections fell to 425,668 metric tons in the week ended August 20, from 514,363 tons a week earlier and well below the 1.05 million tons inspected during the same week last year. Meanwhile, the US spring wheat harvest was advancing rapidly, with 62% of the crop in the bin as of Sunday, compared with 41% a week earlier and above the five-year average of 52%.

### 2026-08-26 19:54:43  #其他

【上海市委網信辦組織召開年中網路安全工作協調機制（擴大）會議】8月25日，上海市委網信辦組織召開年中網路安全工作協調機制（擴大）會議，分析研判當前網路安全形勢，通報上半年全市網路安全風險隱患處置情況，總結重點專項行動推進成效，部署下一階段重點工作任務。各區委網信辦、相關委辦區域性門負責同志參加會議。會議指出，當前網路安全形勢複雜嚴峻，各類網路攻擊、漏洞隱患、資料安全風險多發頻發，網路安全防控壓力持續加大。依託全市網路安全工作協調機制，各成員單位協同聯動、同向發力，上半年精準排查處置多起網路安全隱患，有效防範化解各類網路安全風險，全市網路安全態勢總體平穩可控，堅決守住了不發生重大網路安全事件的底線。會議通報了“提升企業網路安全防護能力”專項行動推進情況。本次行動聚焦強化企業網路安全意識、壓實主體責任、補齊行業短板，面向中小微企業、重點產業、民生服務等重點領域，透過合規宣講、隱患排查、網安義診、技術幫扶等精準舉措，指導企業健全安全管理制度、升級技術防護措施、規範應急處置流程，推動企業網路安全防護由被動應對向主動防禦轉變。下一步，將健全常態化幫扶機制，最佳化安全服務供給，全面提升企業網路安全整體防護水平。會議總結了“網路安全培訓進街鎮”專項行動成效。該行動旨在推動網路安全防線向基層延伸，切實提升基層一線工作人員風險識別、隱患排查和應急處置能力。目前，已完成全市16個區的18場培訓，共計1685家單位、企業，2020人次參訓。下一步將深化“以練促學、以訓促建”，持續拓寬培訓覆蓋面，暢通網路安全培訓“最後一公里”，切實築牢基層網路安全防線。會議還對第48屆世界技能大賽、第九屆進博會網路安全服務保障，網路資料安全風險評估及個人資訊保護等工作作出部署。（澎湃）

【上半年國內聚變能融資總額規模達80億，同比猛增約233倍】8月26日，在2026核聚變能大會暨核聚變活動週上，科技部三司三處處長郭育敏披露，今年上半年核聚變能領域創業投資異常活躍。據不完全統計，國內聚變能一級市場融資事件約30筆，同比增長約8倍；融資總額規模達80億元，同比增幅超233倍。近30家初創企業在此期間獲得資本加持；產業鏈上下游企業同步受益，合計融資約15億元。郭育敏指出，當前聚變投資呈現“大額化”與“連續化”並行特徵，單筆融資額屢創新高，投資主體日趨多元。同時，社會資本正從過去單純資助科研專案，加速轉向商業化聚變企業；技術路線覆蓋高溫超導託卡馬克、球形託卡馬克等多個方向，百花齊放；投資階段也已從早期技術驗證延伸至工程驗證，行業正邁向更成熟的發展週期。

US Mortgage Applications Inch Lower for 2nd Week — The volume of mortgage applications in the US fell by 1% in the third week of August, extending the soft 0.4% drop in the earlier period, according to data compiled by the Mortgage Bankers Association. The slight change was aligned with a muted bps increase in the average benchmark 30-year fixed mortgage rate relatively close to the highest level in one year, as soaring levels of corporate debt, widening deficit spending by the federal government, and concerns of a complacent Federal Reserve drove yields on the 30-year bond to a multi-decade high. Applications to refinance a mortgage, which are more sensitive to short-term changes in interest rates, fell 2%.

【Ukraine says it struck Russia's Norsi refinery, causing fire】Ukraine said its armed forces struck the Norsi refinery in Russia's Volga region, triggering a fire. The General Staff said the strike hit a large refinery near Nizhny Novgorod and that "operations against enemy critical targets continue." Norsi's design capacity is about 340,000 bpd, making it one of Russia's largest refineries; it is roughly 440 km from Moscow and primarily supplies the capital and surrounding high-demand fuel region. Ukraine said the attack is the latest in a wave of intensified strikes on Russia's oil-processing sector.

### 2026-08-26 19:09:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Mortgage Rate #Importance 2

US Mortgage Rates Hold Near One-Year Highs — The average contract rate for a 30-year fixed mortgage in the US edged up to 6.78% in the week ending August 21, 2026, from 6.77% the previous week, remaining near the one-year highs reached in late July, according to the Mortgage Bankers Association. The increase followed a rise in Treasury yields as persistent inflation concerns continued to weigh on bond markets, despite the US Treasury Department’s unexpected announcement of a larger bond buyback last week, which had been expected to support demand for government debt. Mortgage rates have climbed nearly 70 bps since the US and Israel launched strikes against Iran in late February, as higher oil prices fueled inflation fears and reinforced expectations that the Federal Reserve will keep rates higher for longer. Total mortgage applications fell 1.0% for a second straight week. Refinancing applications dropped 2.0%, while purchase applications declined 0.3%.

【Nepal flash floods kill at least 16, 384 tourists missing; icefall-dam breach suspected】On August 26, flash floods struck Rasuwa district in northern Nepal’s Bagmati province, sweeping villages, markets and infrastructure. The Nepal Tourism Board said at least 16 people have died and 384 tourists are missing, including 93 Nepalis and the remainder foreign nationals. Scientists’ preliminary assessment is that an icefall blocked the Lende River, forming a temporary dam that later breached; investigators say the cause remains under review. Damage to roads, bridges, communications and power has hampered rescue operations and water levels in the mountains remain high, delaying a full accounting of casualties and property losses. A local resident said the event near the Nepal–China border may be the largest in decades and could be linked to glacier melt.

### 2026-08-26 18:58:27  #宏觀 #兩會

【全國政協十四屆常委會第十八次會議閉幕 王滬寧主持並講話】
政協第十四屆全國委員會常務委員會第十八次會議26日下午在京閉幕，會議以“加大保障和改善民生力度，紮實推進全體人民共同富裕”為議題協商議政。中共中央政治局常委、全國政協主席王滬寧主持閉幕會並講話。他表示，中共十八大以來，以習近平同志為核心的中共中央堅持以人民為中心的發展思想，部署打贏人類歷史上規模最大的脫貧攻堅戰，建成世界上規模最大的教育體系、社會保障體系、醫療衛生體系、城市住房保障體系，全方位、持續性地提高人民生活品質、增強群眾獲得感幸福感安全感，民生事業發展取得歷史性成就，全體人民共同富裕邁出堅實步伐。我們要深刻領悟“兩個確立”的決定性意義、堅決做到“兩個維護”，堅持人民政協為人民，協助黨和政府增進人民福祉、穩步推動全體人民共同富裕，為加快推進中國式現代化廣泛凝聚人心、凝聚共識、凝聚智慧、凝聚力量。
王滬寧表示，共同富裕是社會主義現代化的重要目標，中國式現代化，民生為大。人民政協要深入學習領會習近平總書記關於加大保障和改善民生力度、紮實推進全體人民共同富裕的重要論述，圍繞中共二十屆四中全會《建議》和“十五五”規劃綱要相關任務部署，緊扣加強“十五五”時期民生建設、促進高質量充分就業、完善收入分配製度、辦好人民滿意的教育、健全社會保障體系、加快建設健康中國等方面問題，組織政協委員多建利民之言、多做惠民之事，助推黨和國家決策部署落地見效，讓現代化建設成果更多更公平惠及全體人民。要高質量完成全國政協全年工作任務，持續抓好協商議政工作，認真辦好重要紀念和團結聯誼活動。（新華社）

【受“沙德爾”影響 浙江海事局啟動一級防颱應急響應】今年第18號颱風“沙德爾”（強颱風級）的中心今天（26日）下午5時位於浙閩交界東偏北方向約600公里的海面上，就是北緯28.4度，東經126.4度，中心附近最大風力14級（42米/秒），中心最低氣壓為955百帕，七級風圈半徑350-400公里，十級風圈半徑120-150公里，十二級風圈半徑50-60公里。根據“沙德爾”最新動態，浙江海事局決定於今天18時啟動一級防颱應急響應，溫州市水上防颱分指揮部決定於今天18時啟動水上防颱一級應急響應。截至目前，浙江沿海146個水上工程專案已全部停工，527艘施工船舶正陸續前往安全避風水域，浙江全省162條客渡運航線在今天14時已經全部停航。浙江海事部門已累計釋出預警資訊11.8萬條，點驗船舶4萬艘次，動態部署8艘大馬力拖輪應急待命，確保快速處置各類突發事件。（央視新聞）

【小天才公關部回應體重判定爭議】近日，有網友在社交平臺曬出小天才電話手錶健康監測功能截圖。截圖顯示，一名身高167cm、體重50.5kg的女孩，被手錶判定為“偏重”，標註“體重超過99%的同齡女生”。該內容引發網友廣泛討論，不少網友擔憂，這樣的判定可能會誤導未成年人，催生體重焦慮。8月26日，小天才公關部工作人員回應，小天才已於8月25日當晚完成緊急線上整改，目前已經移除所有評價、判定類內容，產品頁面僅保留使用者手動錄入的身高、體重，以及透過標準公式計算出的BMI三項客觀資料。（中國青年報）

### 2026-08-26 18:46:14  #Trading Economics #Markets #Germany #Stock Market #Importance 1

DAX Rebounds Slightly — The DAX 40 turned slightly positive, trading around 26,300 on Wednesday and heading for a second straight session of gains. Market sentiment was supported by the ongoing decline in oil prices amid hopes for the reopening of the Strait of Hormuz, alongside stabilizing bond yields. However, a sense of caution prevailed ahead of Nvidia’s earnings and key US inflation data that could influence rate expectations. Banks led sectoral gains, with tech, industrials, pharma and healthcare, utilities, and chemical stocks also advancing, offsetting sharp losses in software. Deutsche Bank and Commerzbank moved to the top of the index, surging 4.3% and 2.9%, respectively.

【中國氣象局升級重大氣象災害為Ⅱ級應急響應狀態】根據中央氣象臺最新預計，今年第18號颱風“沙德爾”將於27日夜間至28日上午在浙江象山到福建連江一帶沿海登陸，浙閩贛湘粵桂及雲貴川等地將自東向西出現強降雨，部分地區累計降雨量200～400毫米，浙江和福建局地超過500毫米。26日至28日，青海、陝西、甘肅、山西、河北、山東等地部分地區有大到暴雨，局地大暴雨，並伴有雷暴大風、冰雹等強對流天氣。中央氣象臺於8月26日18時繼續釋出颱風橙色、暴雨黃色、強對流天氣藍色預警。此外，西藏日喀則市吉隆縣吉隆口岸尼泊爾一側遭受泥石流災害，目前正處於救援關鍵期。經綜合研判和應急會商，中國氣象局今天升級重大氣象災害（颱風、暴雨、強對流）為Ⅱ級應急響應狀態。（央視新聞）

### 2026-08-26 18:30:29  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #DXY #Impact bullish #WTI #US10Y

【Central bankers converge on Jackson Hole; inflation, higher-rate risks to dominate】Global central bankers meet in Jackson Hole this week to reassess inflation and the risk that renewed supply shocks could push borrowing costs higher. Thin Ice Macro economist Spiros said policymakers will likely err on the side of caution, treating inflation as the most feared risk because supply shocks can occur unexpectedly. SOCGEN US head of research Subhadra Rajappa said countries’ exposure to energy-price swings differs with import dependence, leaving Europe and Japan more sensitive to Middle East developments and oil. Goldman Sachs’ chief Europe economist said policy rates in the US and UK remain restrictive, giving the Fed and Bank of England more time to observe how energy-driven shocks evolve. Former Philadelphia Fed president Patrick Harker said the Iran war has shifted policymakers’ framing and choices, and that the global economy is now facing multiple simultaneous supply shocks.

UK Retail Activity Slumps in August: CBI — The CBI’s headline sales balance fell to -48 in August 2026 from -26 in July, well below market expectations of -24. Retailers reported significantly lower sales volumes, while the broader distribution sector also faced subdued trading conditions. The deterioration weighed on hiring and investment plans, although retailers expect some improvement in September, with sales expectations rising to -22, the strongest outlook since March. Sales for the time of year also declined, reaching -26 from -18 in July. The CBI’s quarterly business situation balance weakened to -29 from -15 in May, highlighting continued pressure on the sector. However, investment intentions improved considerably to -16 from -52 in May, their strongest level since February 2024. Employment trends also showed some resilience, with retailers cutting jobs at the slowest pace since November 2025.

North Macedonia Jobless Rate Holds Steady — North Macedonia’s unemployment rate stood at 11.3% in the second quarter of 2026, remaining unchanged from the prior quarter which reached the lowest level since at least 1993. The number of unemployed declined by 1.5% to 90,020, of whom 63.6% were men and 36.4% women. Meanwhile, the number of employed persons edged up by 0.2% to 708,698, with men accounting for 57.7% and women for 42.3%. The employment rate edged up to 46.6%, while the labor force participation rate stood at 52.5%. Informal employment rose 7.2% to 101,022, with the largest share concentrated in agriculture, forestry and fisheries (51%), followed by construction (17.2%).

【國家藥監局：對法國PIERRE FABRE MEDICAMENT恩考芬尼膠囊部分批次暫停銷售、使用】國家藥監局近期組織對法國PIERRE FABRE MEDICAMENT恩考芬尼膠囊（英文名稱：Encorafenib Capsules；註冊證號：國藥準字HJ20250082；生產地址：14 Schoolhouse Road, Somerset, NJ, 08873-1213, United States；Site Progipharm, rue du Lycée, 45500, GIEN, France）開展遠端檢查。經查，該製劑的註冊資訊所載明的原料藥恩考芬尼生產工藝發生重大變更尚未獲批准，企業將該原料藥用於部分批次製劑（批號5G2T3、5G2T4）生產並上市放行銷售至中國市場，質量管理體系的變更控制和放行稽核與藥品生產質量管理規範存在嚴重偏離，不符合我國《藥品生產質量管理規範（2010年修訂）》及附錄要求。根據《中華人民共和國藥品管理法》第九十九條、《藥品醫療器械境外檢查管理規定》第三十條等有關規定，國家藥監局決定，自即日起，暫停銷售和使用批號為5G2T3、5G2T4的PIERRE FABRE MEDICAMENT恩考芬尼膠囊。

### 2026-08-26 18:04:02  #公司

【順豐被曝“變相降薪”？月薪15%劃為季度績效，官方回應：非降薪，旨在完善員工激勵機制，不涉及一二線員工】近日，有多位順豐員工在社交平臺發文稱，收到公司郵件通知調整薪酬結構，將固定月薪的15%劃為季度績效。有員工認為是“變相降薪”、“付押金上班”。對此調整，順豐官方回應稱，集團本次薪酬結構最佳化調整並非降薪，旨在持續完善員工激勵機制，實現業績與員工收益協同發展，調整後公司整體薪酬支出將略有增加。此次調整是將年度現金薪酬的15%拆分作為季度考核基數，調整後將會有10%以上的績優員工收入增加，實現員工優績優酬。另外，此次調整僅針對總部職能，不涉及一、二線員工。（新浪科技）

### 2026-08-26 18:00:24  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China

【Shanghai launches municipal post-investment empowerment platform for state-capital funds】Aug. 26 — Shanghai’s municipal post-investment empowerment collaboration platform for state-owned capital funds began operations. Shanghai plans state-capital fund scale above 660 billion yuan by end-2025. The platform, led by the Shanghai SASAC and Shanghai Guotou, will pilot joint “empowerment” consortia to provide full-chain, closed-loop raise-invest-manage-exit fund services aimed at supporting sci‑tech company growth. The first consortium includes Xuhui and Changning districts and 11 SOE and financial members — Shanghai Guotou, Shanghai International Group, Shanghai Electric, Shanghai Instrumentation, Shanghai Tunnel Co., Shanghai Pudong Development Bank (SPD Bank), Bank of Shanghai, Shanghai Rural Commercial Bank, Guotai Haitong, Lingang Group and Shanghai Inspection Company. The consortia will expand to cover all 16 districts and recruit more professional asset managers, industry players and park operators.

### 2026-08-26 17:54:41  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #Treasury Yields #USDJPY #Impact bearish #US10Y #Impact bullish #DXY

【Timeline of Bessent market interventions】Under Bessent, key market interventions since early 2025 included: Early 2025 — initiated FX quote inquiries with banks for yen bids to probe market views ahead of possible intervention. June 2025 — executed a one‑off Treasury repo of $10.0bn, the largest in Treasury history, to improve bond market liquidity and buffer US note sell‑offs; markets read it as groundwork for larger repos. Oct. 2025 — directly bought Argentine pesos in excess of $1.0bn ahead of Oct. 26 midterm elections, stabilising the peso and reversing declines in Argentine equities and bonds. Oct. 2025 — agreed a $20.0bn currency‑swap framework with the Central Bank of Argentina to provide dollar liquidity. Nov. 2025 — signalled gradual adjustments to coupon Treasury auction sizes to avoid market disruption while emphasising auctions would remain regular and predictable; no immediate change to sizes. July 2026 — led the first direct US purchases of yen in nearly 30 years via Goldman Sachs and MORGAN STANLEY selling euros to buy yen, sized $5–10bn, aimed at arresting sharp yen depreciation and preventing Japanese sales of Treasuries that could lift US yields; yen rallied from ~164 to ~157. Aug. 2026 — doubled 10–30yr Treasury repo operations from $20bn to at least $40bn per operation from Sept. 9 to push down long‑end yields as the 30‑yr approached 5.3%; the 30‑yr yield fell about 10bps, US equity indices closed higher and the Bloomberg Dollar Spot touched a three‑month low. Aug. 2026 — continued to signal potential further repo expansion and considered using roughly $950bn–$1.0trn of Treasury General Account (TGA) balances to back operations, aiming to further restrain long‑end yields and remove funding‑source uncertainty; this prompted modest additional declines in long‑end yields.

### 2026-08-26 17:50:00  #公司

【一超之上 再啟新局，伊利從規模引領邁向價值深耕】8月26日，伊利股份（600887.SH）釋出2026年中報。上半年，公司實現營業總收入644.9億元，同比增長4.13%；核心經營利潤83.8億元，同比增長10%；營收、核心經營利潤高位雙增，穩居亞洲乳業第一。同期，公司液體乳、奶粉及奶製品、冷飲全線業務全面增長，均穩居行業第一。
乳業“一超”的領先，早已超越規模本身，更是以價值為核心，以經營質量、均衡業務結構、創新能力和前瞻佈局為支撐的體系性領先。正是這種更高維度的領先，讓伊利能夠始終圍繞消費者健康需求，以戰略前瞻開啟長期空間，以戰略定力推動目標兌現，以龍頭韌性與擔當引領行業穿越週期。
一超之上，再啟新局。錨定乳品深加工、精準營養等戰略業務，伊利正以行業領導者的先見先行，推動產業鏈向更高價值躍遷，實現從規模引領到價值深耕的戰略躍遷。
公司以功能營養為核心發展方向，持續推動成人奶粉及功能保健食品的功效研發、應用及產品儲備。公司全面佈局控糖、骨骼、心臟、免疫等功能賽道及羊奶粉、駝奶粉等細分功能產品，覆蓋消費者多元營養健康需求。
上半年，“欣活”骨能系列奶粉表現亮眼，帶動品牌實現雙位數增長；“倍暢”品牌推出益生菌富硒高鈣羊奶粉，帶動品牌實現20%以上增長。尼爾森與星圖資料綜合顯示，伊利成人奶粉業務市場份額提升至26.8%，持續位居行業第一。
報告期，在不斷滿足消費者需求的努力中，公司的功能保健品業務也已經蔚然成型。上半年，“伊利健康科學”面向代謝健康營養需求人群上市纖控益生菌凍乾粉，採用K56專利菌株作為核心原料，含有1000億活性益生菌，並復配多種植物成分，帶動功能保健食品業務翻倍增長。
乳深加工是精準功能性營養髮展的重要依託，亦是撬動中國乳業轉型升級的關鍵支點。伊利也早已對乳深加工領域作出戰略性佈局。公司現代智慧健康谷5G乳酪全球智造標杆基地，日處理鮮牛奶600噸，涵蓋稀奶油、多種乳酪及乳鐵蛋白粉等生產線。
上半年，隨著乳深加工工藝的突破和系列專案產線的落地投產，伊利在高價值乳品原料領域的佈局進一步深化，極大提升公司營養品供應鏈的自主權，科學營養和專業餐飲業務的發展也將得到有效支援。伊利營養價值創造“功力倍增”，一片新藍海，正徐徐展開。

### 2026-08-26 17:48:15  #Trading Economics #Economy #Iceland #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Iceland Producer Inflation Eases from 4-Year High — Iceland’s producer prices rose by 18.6% year-on-year in July 2026, slowing from a four-year high of 19.4% in the previous month. Price growth eased for metal products (38.9% vs 45.3% in June), food production (3.6% vs 3.9%), products sold domestically (2.0% vs 2.3%), exported products (26.9% vs 27.9%), and exported products excluding marine products (29.8% vs 32.0%). Meanwhile, costs increased at a faster pace for marine products, climbing to 22.5% from 21.9% in June, as well as for other manufacturing industries, jumping to 6.1% from 4.1%. On a monthly basis, producer prices fell 0.5% in July 2026, reversing a 1.6% gain in the previous month and marking the first decline since June 2025, as well as the lowest monthly reading since then.

### 2026-08-26 17:42:52  #MKT News #XAUUSD #Impact bullish #DXY #Impact mixed

Iran's equity rally is best read as an asset re-pricing in a high-inflation environment, not a traditional fundamentals-driven bull market. Key market signals to watch: whether TEDPIX can hold above 6,000,000; whether the rial continues to weaken; whether inflation remains near 90%; whether gold/USD keeps rising; and whether real profits at export-oriented petrochemical, steel and mining firms improve. If stocks rise while the rial stabilizes, inflation falls and corporate earnings recover, that would signal a substantive economic rebound. If instead stocks rise alongside a weaker rial, higher gold and inflation stuck near 90%, the rally is likely driven by cash flight and hedging of currency depreciation and inflation rather than underlying economic repair.

### 2026-08-26 17:37:58  #MKT News #XAUUSD #Impact bullish

【Why Iran's equity market has suddenly strengthened】Iran's rally reflects post-conflict asset revaluation, very high inflation expectations and liquidity returning to equities rather than a pickup in real fundamentals. The Tehran Stock Exchange reopened in late May after an ~80‑day suspension following US‑Israeli strikes; TEDPIX, which was around 3.7m points before the halt, has climbed past 6.38m. Inflation remains extreme — Tindex shows July inflation near 87.9% — prompting a shift out of cash into stocks, gold, property and FX, which can drive strong nominal equity gains without corresponding rises in real purchasing power or corporate profits. Many listed firms (petrochemicals, steel, copper, mining, energy and exporters) have revenues or assets linked to dollars or commodity prices, so rial depreciation and inflation can boost nominal revenues and prompt asset revaluation. Market breadth is wide: over 90% of stocks are up and more than 80% have risen 2–3%, pointing to a liquidity‑driven, market‑wide buying wave rather than a narrow, sector‑specific rally.
